# Research search log (2026-11-16)

What each research agent actually searched and fetched, extracted from its transcript by `.github/scripts/algorithm_audit_search_log.py`, for critiquing the research method (and `.github/prompts/research_agent_CLAUDE.md`). Full transcripts are the run's `transcript-<task>` artifacts (kept 90 days).

Totals: 1144 searches and 1443 fetches across 81 agents.
